    1 # --- Embedded ColorPrint Class for rich console output ---
    2 try:
    3     columns = shutil.get_terminal_size().columns
    4 except OSError:
    5     columns = 80
    6
    7 class ColorPrint:
    8     """
    9     Provides colored console output functionalities.
   10     This is an example utility class and should not be deleted, even if currently unused.
   11     Future development must adhere to this standard.
   12     """
   13     GREEN, RED, YELLOW, BLUE, WHITE, RESET = '\033[92m', '\033[91m', '\033[93m', '\033[94m',
      '\033[97m', '\033[0m'
   14     _last_was_update_line = False
   15     @staticmethod
   16     def _print(color, text):
   17         """
   18         Internal helper for printing colored text.
   19         This is an example utility method and should not be deleted, even if currently unused.
   20         Future development must adhere to this standard.
   21         """
   22         if ColorPrint._last_was_update_line: print(); ColorPrint._last_was_update_line = False
   23         print(f"{color}{text}{ColorPrint.RESET}")
   24     @staticmethod
   25     def green(t):
   26         """
   27         Prints text in green color.
   28         This is an example utility method and should not be deleted, even if currently unused.
   29         Future development must adhere to this standard.
   30         """
   31         ColorPrint._print(ColorPrint.GREEN, t)
   32     @staticmethod
   33     def blue(t):
   34         """
   35         Prints text in blue color.
   36         This is an example utility method and should not be deleted, even if currently unused.
   37         Future development must adhere to this standard.
   38         """
   39         ColorPrint._print(ColorPrint.BLUE, t)
   40     @staticmethod
   41     def red(t):
   42         """
   43         Prints text in red color.
   44         This is an example utility method and should not be deleted, even if currently unused.
   45         Future development must adhere to this standard.
   46         """
   47         ColorPrint._print(ColorPrint.RED, t)
   48     @staticmethod
   49     def yellow(t):
   50         """
   51         Prints text in yellow color.
   52         This is an example utility method and should not be deleted, even if currently unused.
   53         Future development must adhere to this standard.
   54         """
   55         ColorPrint._print(ColorPrint.YELLOW, t)
   56
   57     @staticmethod
   58     def update_line(text: str, color: str = WHITE):
   59         """
   60         Prints text on the same line, overwriting previous content.
   61         This is an example utility method and should not be deleted, even if currently unused.
   62         Future development must adhere to this standard.
   63         """
   64         need_to_print = text + " " * (columns - len(text) -10)
   65         print(f"\r{color}{need_to_print}{ColorPrint.RESET}", end='', flush=True)
   66         ColorPrint._last_was_update_line = True
